{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M78LF4dukjJHHp14c4ri5D","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\timport {\n4\t  convertProgramToProtocolV2,\n5\t  convertV1ProtocolToV2,\n6\t  resolveUniqueName,\n7\t  MIGRATED_PROGRAM_MARKER,\n8\t  MIGRATED_V1_PROTOCOL_MARKER,\n9\t  type ConvertedProtocol,\n10\t  type FeedRef,\n11\t  type LegacyProgramRow,\n12\t  type LegacyV1ProtocolRow,\n13\t  type SpeciesRef,\n14\t  type V1Enrichment,\n15\t} from '../../feeding-protocol/migration/legacy-protocol-conversion';\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV2 (Faz 4 — plan §9.1–9.3, K-3, D-14)\n19\t *\n20\t * Veri taşıma #1: legacy FeedingProgram / v1 FeedingProtocol tanımları ve tank\n21\t * atamaları FeedingProtocolV2 modeline taşınır. Dönüşüm matematiği SAF lib'de\n22\t * (`feeding-protocol/migration/legacy-protocol-conversion.ts`, birim testli);\n23\t * bu dosya yalnız okuma/yazma sıhhiyesi taşır.\n24\t *\n25\t *  - Programlar: ACTIVE → v2 ACTIVE; draft/paused → DRAFT; completed/cancelled\n26\t *    taşınmaz. Band oran/FCR'ı feed matrisinden band-orta ağırlık +\n27\t *    tenant-medyan sıcaklıkta örneklenir; tanklardaki batch v1 protokol\n28\t *    taşıyorsa öğün/sıcaklık/oran oradan zenginleştirilir.\n29\t *  - v1 protokoller: feedId çözülemeyen bantlar → DRAFT (migrationNote'lu);\n30\t *    `preMedicationFasting` settings.adjustments'a aynen taşınır.\n31\t *  - Atamalar (K-3): migrate edilen TÜM atamalar `paused` yaratılır — Faz 5\n32\t *    motoru deploy olduğunda prod'da aktif v2 ataması YOKTUR (çift planlama\n33\t *    imkânsız); Faz 6 cutover migration'ı operatör kapısıyla aktive eder.\n34\t *  - Sitesi çözülemeyen üniteye atama YAZILMAZ (SEC-HIGH-051 fail-closed\n35\t *    duruşu); D-14 mutabakat scripti bu üniteleri sayar/listeler.\n36\t *\n37\t * İdempotency: protokoller migrationNote başındaki `[migrated:...:<id>]`\n38\t * işaretiyle, atamalar (tenantId, unitId) üzerindeki mevcut active/paused\n39\t * atama kontrolüyle korunur — tekrar koşum çift kayıt üretmez.\n40\t *\n41\t * DDL yok; tüm ifadeler ŞEMA-NİTELEMESİZ ve her pass kendi şemasının verisini\n42\t * işler (tenant fan-out disiplini — CreateFeedingProtocolV2Tables ile aynı).\n43\t */\n44\texport class MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n45\t  name = 'MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000';\n46\t\n47\t  /** Migration'ın yazdığı atamaların ayırt edici createdBy işareti (rapor + idempotency). */\n48\t  static readonly ASSIGNMENT_SENTINEL = '00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000f4';\n49\t\n50\t  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n51\t    await queryRunner.query(`SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'`);\n52\t    await queryRunner.query(`SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '300s'`);\n53\t\n54\t    // ── Bağlam yükleri (pass'in kendi şeması) ────────────────────────────────\n55\t    const feeds: Array<FeedRef & { tenantId: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n56\t      `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", code, name, type, \"feedingMatrix2D\" FROM \"feeds\" WHERE \"isDeleted\" = false`,\n57\t    );\n58\t    const speciesRows: Array<SpeciesRef & { tenantId: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n59\t      `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", \"commonName\", \"scientificName\", \"localName\", \"growthStages\" FROM \"species\"`,\n60\t    );\n61\t    const v1Protocols: LegacyV1ProtocolRow[] = await queryRunner.query(\n62\t      `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", name, description, \"feedId\", species, \"targetFcr\",\n63\t              \"minDissolvedOxygen\", \"temperatureRanges\", \"growthStageProtocols\",\n64\t              \"defaultSchedule\", \"isDefault\"\n65\t       FROM \"feeding_protocols\" WHERE \"isActive\" = true`,\n66\t    );\n67\t    const programs: LegacyProgramRow[] = await queryRunner.query(\n68\t      `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", name, description, status, \"feedAssignments\", \"fcrTable\", settings\n69\t       FROM \"feeding_programs\"\n70\t       WHERE \"isDeleted\" = false AND status IN ('draft', 'active', 'paused')`,\n71\t    );\n72\t    const programTanks: Array<{\n73\t      tenantId: string;\n74\t      feedingProgramId: string;\n75\t      equipmentId: string;\n76\t      equipmentType: 'tank' | 'pond' | 'cage';\n77\t      equipmentName: string;\n78\t      equipmentCode: string;\n79\t      currentFeedId: string | null;\n80\t    }> = await queryRunner.query(\n81\t      `SELECT \"tenantId\", \"feedingProgramId\", \"equipmentId\", \"equipmentType\",\n82\t              \"equipmentName\", \"equipmentCode\", \"currentFeedId\"\n83\t       FROM \"feeding_program_tanks\"\n84\t       WHERE \"isActive\" = true AND \"removedAt\" IS NULL`,\n85\t    );\n86\t    const medianTemps: Array<{ tenantId: string; median: string | number | null }> =\n87\t      await queryRunner.query(\n88\t        `SELECT \"tenantId\", percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY temperature) AS median\n89\t         FROM \"water_quality_measurements\"\n90\t         WHERE temperature IS NOT NULL AND \"measuredAt\" >= now() - interval '90 days'\n91\t         GROUP BY \"tenantId\"`,\n92\t      );\n93\t    const existingV2: Array<{ id: string; tenantId: string; name: string; migrationNote: string | null }> =\n94\t      await queryRunner.query(\n95\t        `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", name, \"migrationNote\" FROM \"feeding_protocols_v2\"`,\n96\t      );\n97\t    // Program → tanklarındaki birincil batch'lerin v1 protokolü (zenginleştirme).\n98\t    const programBatchProtocols: Array<{\n99\t      feedingProgramId: string;\n100\t      protocolId: string;\n101\t      cnt: string | number;\n102\t    }> = await queryRunner.query(\n103\t      `SELECT fpt.\"feedingProgramId\", b.\"protocolId\", COUNT(*) AS cnt\n104\t       FROM \"feeding_program_tanks\" fpt\n105\t       JOIN \"tank_batches\" tb ON tb.\"tankId\" = fpt.\"equipmentId\" AND tb.\"tenantId\" = fpt.\"tenantId\"\n106\t       JOIN \"batches_v2\" b ON b.id = tb.\"primaryBatchId\"\n107\t       WHERE fpt.\"isActive\" = true AND fpt.\"removedAt\" IS NULL\n108\t         AND b.\"protocolId\" IS NOT NULL AND b.\"isActive\" = true\n109\t       GROUP BY fpt.\"feedingProgramId\", b.\"protocolId\"`,\n110\t    );\n111\t    // Batch.protocolId → batch'in balıklı üniteleri (atama türetimi, plan §9.3).\n112\t    const batchUnits: Array<{\n113\t      tenantId: string;\n114\t      protocolId: string;\n115\t      unitId: string;\n116\t      unitName: string | null;\n117\t      unitCode: string | null;\n118\t    }> = await queryRunner.query(\n119\t      `SELECT tb.\"tenantId\", b.\"protocolId\", tb.\"tankId\" AS \"unitId\",\n120\t              tb.\"tankName\" AS \"unitName\", tb.\"tankCode\" AS \"unitCode\"\n121\t       FROM \"tank_batches\" tb\n122\t       JOIN \"batches_v2\" b ON b.id = tb.\"primaryBatchId\"\n123\t       WHERE b.\"protocolId\" IS NOT NULL AND b.\"isActive\" = true AND tb.\"totalQuantity\" > 0`,\n124\t    );\n125\t\n126\t    // ── İndeksler ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n127\t    const feedsByTenant = new Map<string, Map<string, FeedRef>>();\n128\t    const feedsByTypeByTenant = new Map<string, Map<string, FeedRef[]>>();\n129\t    for (const feedRow of feeds) {\n130\t      const byId = feedsByTenant.get(feedRow.tenantId) ?? new Map<string, FeedRef>();\n131\t      byId.set(feedRow.id, feedRow);\n132\t      feedsByTenant.set(feedRow.tenantId, byId);\n133\t      const byType = feedsByTypeByTenant.get(feedRow.tenantId) ?? new Map<string, FeedRef[]>();\n134\t      const bucket = byType.get(feedRow.type) ?? [];\n135\t      bucket.push(feedRow);\n136\t      byType.set(feedRow.type, bucket);\n137\t      feedsByTypeByTenant.set(feedRow.tenantId, byType);\n138\t    }\n139\t    const speciesByTenant = new Map<string, Array<SpeciesRef & { tenantId: string }>>();\n140\t    for (const row of speciesRows) {\n141\t      const bucket = speciesByTenant.get(row.tenantId) ?? [];\n142\t      bucket.push(row);\n143\t      speciesByTenant.set(row.tenantId, bucket);\n144\t    }\n145\t    const medianByTenant = new Map<string, number>();\n146\t    for (const row of medianTemps) {\n147\t      const value = row.median === null ? null : Number(row.median);\n148\t      if (value !== null && Number.isFinite(value)) medianByTenant.set(row.tenantId, value);\n149\t    }\n150\t    const v1ById = new Map(v1Protocols.map((protocol) => [protocol.id, protocol]));\n151\t    const takenNamesByTenant = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\n152\t    const migratedMarkerToV2 = new Map<string, string>();\n153\t    for (const row of existingV2) {\n154\t      const names = takenNamesByTenant.get(row.tenantId) ?? new Set<string>();\n155\t      names.add(row.name.toLowerCase());\n156\t      takenNamesByTenant.set(row.tenantId, names);\n157\t      const marker = row.migrationNote?.match(/^\\[migrated:[^\\]]+\\]/)?.[0];\n158\t      if (marker) migratedMarkerToV2.set(`${row.tenantId}:${marker}`, row.id);\n159\t    }\n160\t    const enrichmentByProgram = new Map<string, V1Enrichment>();\n161\t    {\n162\t      const best = new Map<string, { protocolId: string; cnt: number }>();\n163\t      for (const row of programBatchProtocols) {\n164\t        const cnt = Number(row.cnt);\n165\t        const current = best.get(row.feedingProgramId);\n166\t        if (!current || cnt > current.cnt) {\n167\t          best.set(row.feedingProgramId, { protocolId: row.protocolId, cnt });\n168\t        }\n169\t      }\n170\t      for (const [programId, { protocolId }] of best) {\n171\t        const v1 = v1ById.get(protocolId);\n172\t        if (v1) {\n173\t          enrichmentByProgram.set(programId, {\n174\t            temperatureRanges: v1.temperatureRanges,\n175\t            defaultSchedule: v1.defaultSchedule,\n176\t            minDissolvedOxygen: v1.minDissolvedOxygen,\n177\t            growthStageProtocols: v1.growthStageProtocols,\n178\t          });\n179\t        }\n180\t      }\n181\t    }\n182\t\n183\t    const matchSpecies = (tenantId: string, name: string): SpeciesRef | undefined => {\n184\t      const lowered = name.trim().toLowerCase();\n185\t      return (speciesByTenant.get(tenantId) ?? []).find(\n186\t        (candidate) =>\n187\t          candidate.commonName?.toLowerCase() === lowered ||\n188\t          candidate.scientificName?.toLowerCase() === lowered ||\n189\t          candidate.localName?.toLowerCase() === lowered,\n190\t      );\n191\t    };\n192\t\n193\t    const insertProtocol = async (\n194\t      tenantId: string,\n195\t      marker: string,\n196\t      converted: ConvertedProtocol,\n197\t    ): Promise<string | null> => {\n198\t      const existing = migratedMarkerToV2.get(`${tenantId}:${marker}`);\n199\t      if (existing) return existing; // idempotent tekrar koşum\n200\t      const names = takenNamesByTenant.get(tenantId) ?? new Set<string>();\n201\t      const name = resolveUniqueName(converted.name, names, marker);\n202\t      names.add(name.toLowerCase());\n203\t      takenNamesByTenant.set(tenantId, names);\n204\t      const rows: Array<{ id: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n205\t        `INSERT INTO \"feeding_protocols_v2\"\n206\t           (id, \"tenantId\", name, description, \"speciesId\", \"speciesName\", status,\n207\t            bands, \"temperatureAdjustments\", \"defaultMealSchedule\", \"fcrMatrix\",\n208\t            settings, \"isDefault\", \"migrationNote\", version, \"isDeleted\")\n209\t         VALUES (uuid_generate_v4(), $1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::feeding_protocols_v2_status_enum,\n210\t                 $7::jsonb, $8::jsonb, $9::jsonb, $10::jsonb, $11::jsonb, $12, $13, 1, false)\n211\t         RETURNING id`,\n212\t        [\n213\t          tenantId,\n214\t          name,\n215\t          converted.description ?? null,\n216\t          converted.speciesId ?? null,\n217\t          converted.speciesName ?? null,\n218\t          converted.status,\n219\t          JSON.stringify(converted.bands),\n220\t          converted.temperatureAdjustments ? JSON.stringify(converted.temperatureAdjustments) : null,\n221\t          JSON.stringify(converted.defaultMealSchedule),\n222\t          converted.fcrMatrix ? JSON.stringify(converted.fcrMatrix) : null,\n223\t          JSON.stringify(converted.settings),\n224\t          converted.isDefault,\n225\t          converted.migrationNote,\n226\t        ],\n227\t      );\n228\t      const insertedId = rows[0]?.id ?? null;\n229\t      if (insertedId) migratedMarkerToV2.set(`${tenantId}:${marker}`, insertedId);\n230\t      return insertedId;\n231\t    };\n232\t\n233\t    // ── 1) v1 protokoller → v2 ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n234\t    const v1ToV2 = new Map<string, string>(); // v1 protocol id → v2 id\n235\t    for (const protocol of v1Protocols) {\n236\t      const marker = MIGRATED_V1_PROTOCOL_MARKER(protocol.id);\n237\t      const species = matchSpecies(protocol.tenantId, protocol.species ?? '');\n238\t      const converted = convertV1ProtocolToV2(protocol, {\n239\t        feedsById: feedsByTenant.get(protocol.tenantId) ?? new Map(),\n240\t        feedsByType: feedsByTypeByTenant.get(protocol.tenantId) ?? new Map(),\n241\t        species,\n242\t      });\n243\t      const v2Id = await insertProtocol(protocol.tenantId, marker, converted);\n244\t      if (v2Id) v1ToV2.set(protocol.id, v2Id);\n245\t    }\n246\t\n247\t    // ── 2) programlar → v2 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n248\t    const programToV2 = new Map<string, string>();\n249\t    for (const program of programs) {\n250\t      const marker = MIGRATED_PROGRAM_MARKER(program.id);\n251\t      const converted = convertProgramToProtocolV2(program, {\n252\t        feedsById: feedsByTenant.get(program.tenantId) ?? new Map(),\n253\t        medianTempC: medianByTenant.get(program.tenantId) ?? null,\n254\t        enrichment: enrichmentByProgram.get(program.id),\n255\t      });\n256\t      if (!converted) continue;\n257\t      const v2Id = await insertProtocol(program.tenantId, marker, converted);\n258\t      if (v2Id) programToV2.set(program.id, v2Id);\n259\t    }\n260\t\n261\t    // ── 3) atamalar (HEPSİ paused — K-3) ────────────────────────────────────\n262\t    const unitIds = [\n263\t      ...new Set([\n264\t        ...programTanks.map((tank) => tank.equipmentId),\n265\t        ...batchUnits.map((unit) => unit.unitId),\n266\t      ]),\n267\t    ];\n268\t    interface UnitInfo {\n269\t      name: string;\n270\t      code: string;\n271\t      siteId: string | null;\n272\t      category: string | null;\n273\t    }\n274\t    const unitInfo = new Map<string, UnitInfo>();\n275\t    if (unitIds.length > 0) {\n276\t      const rows: Array<{\n277\t        id: string;\n278\t        name: string;\n279\t        code: string;\n280\t        siteId: string | null;\n281\t        category: string | null;\n282\t      }> = await queryRunner.query(\n283\t        `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.code, d.\"siteId\" AS \"siteId\", et.category AS category\n284\t         FROM \"equipment\" e\n285\t         LEFT JOIN \"departments\" d ON d.id = e.\"departmentId\"\n286\t         LEFT JOIN \"equipment_types\" et ON et.id = e.\"equipmentTypeId\"\n287\t         WHERE e.id = ANY($1)`,\n288\t        [unitIds],\n289\t      );\n290\t      for (const row of rows) {\n291\t        unitInfo.set(row.id, {\n292\t          name: row.name,\n293\t          code: row.code,\n294\t          siteId: row.siteId,\n295\t          category: row.category,\n296\t        });\n297\t      }\n298\t    }\n299\t\n300\t    const categoryToUnitType = (category: string | null | undefined): 'tank' | 'pond' | 'cage' => {\n301\t      const lowered = (category ?? '').toLowerCase();\n302\t      return lowered === 'pond' || lowered === 'cage' ? lowered : 'tank';\n303\t    };\n304\t\n305\t    const bandIndexForFeed = async (\n306\t      protocolV2Id: string,\n307\t      feedId: string | null,\n308\t    ): Promise<number | null> => {\n309\t      if (!feedId) return null;\n310\t      const rows: Array<{ idx: number }> = await queryRunner.query(\n311\t        `SELECT (band.ordinality - 1)::int AS idx\n312\t         FROM \"feeding_protocols_v2\" p,\n313\t              jsonb_array_elements(p.bands) WITH ORDINALITY AS band(value, ordinality)\n314\t         WHERE p.id = $1 AND band.value->>'feedId' = $2\n315\t         ORDER BY band.ordinality ASC\n316\t         LIMIT 1`,\n317\t        [protocolV2Id, feedId],\n318\t      );\n319\t      return rows[0]?.idx ?? null;\n320\t    };\n321\t\n322\t    const insertAssignment = async (assignment: {\n323\t      tenantId: string;\n324\t      unitId: string;\n325\t      unitType: 'tank' | 'pond' | 'cage';\n326\t      unitName: string;\n327\t      unitCode: string;\n328\t      siteId: string;\n329\t      protocolId: string;\n330\t      currentFeedId: string | null;\n331\t    }): Promise<void> => {\n332\t      // Mevcut canlı (active/paused) atamayı EZME — operatör kurulumu veya\n333\t      // önceki koşum kazanır (idempotency + güvenli tekrar).\n334\t      await queryRunner.query(\n335\t        `INSERT INTO \"feeding_protocol_assignments\"\n336\t           (id, \"tenantId\", \"unitId\", \"unitType\", \"unitName\", \"unitCode\", \"siteId\",\n337\t            \"protocolId\", status, \"effectiveFrom\", overrides, suspensions,\n338\t            \"currentFeedId\", \"currentBandIndex\", \"totalTransitions\", \"createdBy\", version)\n339\t         SELECT uuid_generate_v4(), $1, $2, $3::feeding_protocol_assignments_unittype_enum,\n340\t                $4, $5, $6, $7, 'paused'::feeding_protocol_assignments_status_enum,\n341\t                CURRENT_DATE, '{}'::jsonb, '[]'::jsonb, $8, $9, 0, $10, 1\n342\t         WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n343\t           SELECT 1 FROM \"feeding_protocol_assignments\"\n344\t           WHERE \"tenantId\" = $1 AND \"unitId\" = $2 AND status IN ('active', 'paused')\n345\t         )`,\n346\t        [\n347\t          assignment.tenantId,\n348\t          assignment.unitId,\n349\t          assignment.unitType,\n350\t          assignment.unitName,\n351\t          assignment.unitCode,\n352\t          assignment.siteId,\n353\t          assignment.protocolId,\n354\t          assignment.currentFeedId,\n355\t          await bandIndexForFeed(assignment.protocolId, assignment.currentFeedId),\n356\t          MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000.ASSIGNMENT_SENTINEL,\n357\t        ],\n358\t      );\n359\t    };\n360\t\n361\t    // 3a) Program tankları → atamalar (denorm ad/kod legacy satırdan hazır).\n362\t    for (const tank of programTanks) {\n363\t      const protocolV2Id = programToV2.get(tank.feedingProgramId);\n364\t      if (!protocolV2Id) continue;\n365\t      const info = unitInfo.get(tank.equipmentId);\n366\t      if (!info?.siteId) continue; // D-14: sitesiz ünite fail-closed atlanır; mutabakat scripti listeler\n367\t      await insertAssignment({\n368\t        tenantId: tank.tenantId,\n369\t        unitId: tank.equipmentId,\n370\t        unitType: tank.equipmentType,\n371\t        unitName: tank.equipmentName || info.name,\n372\t        unitCode: tank.equipmentCode || info.code,\n373\t        siteId: info.siteId,\n374\t        protocolId: protocolV2Id,\n375\t        currentFeedId: tank.currentFeedId,\n376\t      });\n377\t    }\n378\t\n379\t    // 3b) Batch.protocolId → batch'in balıklı üniteleri (atamasızsa).\n380\t    for (const unit of batchUnits) {\n381\t      const protocolV2Id = v1ToV2.get(unit.protocolId);\n382\t      if (!protocolV2Id) continue;\n383\t      const info = unitInfo.get(unit.unitId);\n384\t      if (!info?.siteId) continue; // D-14\n385\t      await insertAssignment({\n386\t        tenantId: unit.tenantId,\n387\t        unitId: unit.unitId,\n388\t        unitType: categoryToUnitType(info.category),\n389\t        unitName: unit.unitName || info.name,\n390\t        unitCode: unit.unitCode || info.code,\n391\t        siteId: info.siteId,\n392\t        protocolId: protocolV2Id,\n393\t        currentFeedId: null,\n394\t      });\n395\t    }\n396\t  }\n397\t\n398\t  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n399\t    // Yalnız bu migration'ın yazdıkları geri alınır (işaretlerle seçilir);\n400\t    // operatörün elle kurduğu v2 kayıtlarına dokunulmaz.\n401\t    await queryRunner.query(\n402\t      `DELETE FROM \"feeding_protocol_assignments\" WHERE \"createdBy\" = $1`,\n403\t      [MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000.ASSIGNMENT_SENTINEL],\n404\t    );\n405\t    await queryRunner.query(\n406\t      `DELETE FROM \"feeding_protocols_v2\" WHERE \"migrationNote\" LIKE '[migrated:%'`,\n407\t    );\n408\t  }\n409\t}\n410\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9ef735c0-3ced-4041-a188-30d901fc073d","uuid":"b46b7849-1689-44ec-a0de-1119045d22ed","timestamp":"2026-10-08T04:19:24.222Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-062d5da63359/apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations/1806300000000-MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV2.ts","content":"import { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport {\n  convertProgramToProtocolV2,\n  convertV1ProtocolToV2,\n  resolveUniqueName,\n  MIGRATED_PROGRAM_MARKER,\n  MIGRATED_V1_PROTOCOL_MARKER,\n  type ConvertedProtocol,\n  type FeedRef,\n  type LegacyProgramRow,\n  type LegacyV1ProtocolRow,\n  type SpeciesRef,\n  type V1Enrichment,\n} from '../../feeding-protocol/migration/legacy-protocol-conversion';\n\n/**\n * MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV2 (Faz 4 — plan §9.1–9.3, K-3, D-14)\n *\n * Veri taşıma #1: legacy FeedingProgram / v1 FeedingProtocol tanımları ve tank\n * atamaları FeedingProtocolV2 modeline taşınır. Dönüşüm matematiği SAF lib'de\n * (`feeding-protocol/migration/legacy-protocol-conversion.ts`, birim testli);\n * bu dosya yalnız okuma/yazma sıhhiyesi taşır.\n *\n *  - Programlar: ACTIVE → v2 ACTIVE; draft/paused → DRAFT; completed/cancelled\n *    taşınmaz. Band oran/FCR'ı feed matrisinden band-orta ağırlık +\n *    tenant-medyan sıcaklıkta örneklenir; tanklardaki batch v1 protokol\n *    taşıyorsa öğün/sıcaklık/oran oradan zenginleştirilir.\n *  - v1 protokoller: feedId çözülemeyen bantlar → DRAFT (migrationNote'lu);\n *    `preMedicationFasting` settings.adjustments'a aynen taşınır.\n *  - Atamalar (K-3): migrate edilen TÜM atamalar `paused` yaratılır — Faz 5\n *    motoru deploy olduğunda prod'da aktif v2 ataması YOKTUR (çift planlama\n *    imkânsız); Faz 6 cutover migration'ı operatör kapısıyla aktive eder.\n *  - Sitesi çözülemeyen üniteye atama YAZILMAZ (SEC-HIGH-051 fail-closed\n *    duruşu); D-14 mutabakat scripti bu üniteleri sayar/listeler.\n *\n * İdempotency: protokoller migrationNote başındaki `[migrated:...:<id>]`\n * işaretiyle, atamalar (tenantId, unitId) üzerindeki mevcut active/paused\n * atama kontrolüyle korunur — tekrar koşum çift kayıt üretmez.\n *\n * DDL yok; tüm ifadeler ŞEMA-NİTELEMESİZ ve her pass kendi şemasının verisini\n * işler (tenant fan-out disiplini — CreateFeedingProtocolV2Tables ile aynı).\n */\nexport class MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n  name = 'MigrateFeedingProgramsToProtocolV21806300000000';\n\n  /** Migration'ın yazdığı atamaların ayırt edici createdBy işareti (rapor + idempotency). */\n  static readonly ASSIGNMENT_SENTINEL = NULL`,\n    );\n    const medianTemps: Array<{ tenantId: string; median: string | number | null }> =\n      await queryRunner.query(\n        `SELECT \"tenantId\", percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP \"tenantId\"`,\n      );\n    const existingV2: Array<{ id: string; tenantId: string; name: string; migrationNote: string | null }> =\n      await queryRunner.query(\n        `SELECT id, \"tenantId\", FROM \"feeding_program_tanks\" fpt\n       JOIN \"tank_batches\" tb ON tb.\"tankId\" = fpt.\"equipmentId\" AND tb.\"tenantId\" = fpt.\"tenantId\"\n       JOIN \"batches_v2\" b ON b.id = feedsByTenant.set(feedRow.tenantId, byId);\n      const byType = feedsByTypeByTenant.get(feedRow.tenantId) ?? new Map<string, FeedRef[]>();\n      const bucket = byType.get(feedRow.type) ??
