{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EG4eZAbDkzgGvtZ1kcJsSF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tname: Database Migration Check\n2\t\n3\t# =============================================================================\n4\t# Database Migration Check — TypeORM Migration Validation\n5\t#\n6\t# PURPOSE\n7\t# -------\n8\t# Fast, hermetic validation of the TypeORM migration files that actually\n9\t# ship with this codebase, with no database, no NX build, and no docker.\n10\t# The previous revision of this workflow tried to:\n11\t#\n12\t#   1. Build gateway-api with `npx nx build gateway-api`, then run\n13\t#      `typeorm migration:run -d dist/apps/gateway-api/src/data-source.js`.\n14\t#      Neither gateway-api nor any other service owns a `data-source.ts`\n15\t#      file — migrations are registered as class references inside\n16\t#      `TypeOrmModule.forRoot({ migrations: [...] })` and executed at\n17\t#      service-boot time by `MigrationRunnerService`, not by the typeorm\n18\t#      CLI. The step has never worked in this codebase.\n19\t#\n20\t#   2. Run Flyway validate against `database/migrations/core/**` and\n21\t#      `database/migrations/modules/**`. The Flyway tree is a\n22\t#      vestigial scaffolding experiment that was never wired into\n23\t#      the runtime — nothing in the application code references\n24\t#      `database/migrations/**` (every service registers its own\n25\t#      TypeORM migration classes via TypeOrmModule.forRoot per the\n26\t#      WHAT THIS WORKFLOW CHECKS NOW section below). The Flyway\n27\t#      job was removed when this workflow was rewritten.\n28\t#\n29\t#      DATA-LOW-002 cure: a previous revision of this comment\n30\t#      claimed \"9 of the 13 SQL files in that tree are empty (0\n31\t#      bytes)\". Direct file-size check at 2026-04-29 showed every\n32\t#      `database/migrations/core/V*.sql` carried 281–2491 bytes\n33\t#      and every `database/migrations/modules/<svc>/V*.sql` carried\n34\t#      762–5598 bytes. None empty. The misclaim was a documentation-\n35\t#      rot artifact from a much earlier iteration of the tree —\n36\t#      kept here as a historical note in case future maintainers\n37\t#      grep for the old phrasing.\n38\t#\n39\t#      The Flyway tree itself is left in place so the git history\n40\t#      stays intact and anyone looking for the old Flyway design\n41\t#      can still find it. DATA-HIGH-001 (the agent's separate\n42\t#      tracking finding for the orphan tree) covers the eventual\n43\t#      removal — until that lands, this workflow neither validates\n44\t#      the tree nor relies on it.\n45\t#\n46\t# Beyond that, the `npx nx build` step hung silently for hours on every\n47\t# run because the Windows-generated `package-lock.json` has no\n48\t# `@nx/nx-linux-x64-gnu` binary; without it NX hangs until the 6-hour\n49\t# GitHub Actions runner timeout. Five zombie runs piled up at\n50\t# 09:36Z, 12:27Z, 13:37Z, 13:53Z, and 14:28Z on 2026-04-07 before this\n51\t# rewrite landed.\n52\t#\n53\t# WHAT THIS WORKFLOW CHECKS NOW\n54\t# -----------------------------\n55\t# The real migration path in this codebase is\n56\t# `apps/<service>/src/**/migrations/<timestamp>-<Name>.ts`, one class\n57\t# per file, registered in the service's AppModule. The checks below\n58\t# match that reality:\n59\t#\n60\t#   1. Filename convention — every migration file name must match\n61\t#      `<13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts`.\n62\t#   2. Class name consistency — the exported class inside must be\n63\t#      named `<PascalCaseName><timestamp>` and match the file name.\n64\t#   3. Global timestamp uniqueness — no two migrations anywhere in the\n65\t#      repo may share the same timestamp. Collisions would produce\n66\t#      undefined ordering under TypeORM's migration runner.\n67\t#   4. Syntax / type check — each migration file compiles under\n68\t#      `tsc --noEmit` against the service's `tsconfig.build.json` so\n69\t#      structural errors (missing imports, bad decorator usage, typos\n70\t#      in table names as SQL string literals — the latter caught only\n71\t#      at runtime but the former all caught here) surface at PR time\n72\t#      instead of at deploy time.\n73\t#\n74\t# The job finishes in well under a minute and has no external\n75\t# dependencies: no postgres, no docker, no native binaries, no NX.\n76\t# It cannot hang.\n77\t#\n78\t# Real integration-level migration validation — i.e. \"does the\n79\t# migration actually run against a database\" — happens at deploy time\n80\t# via `MigrationRunnerService.onApplicationBootstrap()` and the\n81\t# deploy workflow's `Verify deployment` health check. Two different\n82\t# quality gates at two different layers, neither duplicating the\n83\t# other's work.\n84\t#\n85\t# SEC-CI-002: All third-party actions pinned to full commit SHAs.\n86\t# =============================================================================\n87\t\n88\ton:\n89\t  pull_request:\n90\t    paths:\n91\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n92\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/*.entity.ts'\n93\t      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n94\t      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n95\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n96\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n97\t      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n98\t  push:\n99\t    branches: [main, develop]\n100\t    paths:\n101\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n102\t      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n103\t      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n104\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n105\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n106\t      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n107\t  # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — nightly run catches drift that touched zero\n108\t  # path-filtered files but introduced runtime drift (e.g. an\n109\t  # init-script change merged through a different filename, or a\n110\t  # silent platform-image bump in docker-compose.infra.yml). 03:00\n111\t  # UTC chosen to follow the post-midnight infra-quiet window in\n112\t  # docs/runbooks/.\n113\t  schedule:\n114\t    - cron: '0 3 * * *'\n115\t\n116\t# SEC-CI-021: Minimum token permissions.\n117\tpermissions:\n118\t  contents: read\n119\t\n120\tjobs:\n121\t  typeorm-migration-validation:\n122\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n123\t    # Bounded budget — the checks take ~30 seconds on a cold runner.\n124\t    # 10 minutes is a generous ceiling that still guarantees no zombies.\n125\t    timeout-minutes: 10\n126\t\n127\t    steps:\n128\t      - name: Checkout repository\n129\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n130\t\n131\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n132\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n133\t        with:\n134\t          node-version: '22'\n135\t\n136\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n137\t      # Check 1 + 2: filename convention and class name consistency.\n138\t      # Pure shell — no node_modules needed. Fails fast on the first bad\n139\t      # file with an inline GitHub annotation so the PR reviewer can\n140\t      # click straight to the offending line.\n141\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n142\t      - name: Validate migration filename and class name conventions\n143\t        run: |\n144\t          set -euo pipefail\n145\t          INVALID=0\n146\t          while IFS= read -r file; do\n147\t            base=$(basename \"$file\")\n148\t            # Expected: <13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts\n149\t            if ! echo \"$base\" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{13}-[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*\\.ts$'; then\n150\t              echo \"::error file=$file::Invalid migration filename: '$base' (expected <13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts)\"\n151\t              INVALID=1\n152\t              continue\n153\t            fi\n154\t\n155\t            # Derive the expected class name from the file name:\n156\t            #   \"1781900000000-ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz.ts\"\n157\t            # → class \"ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz1781900000000\"\n158\t            ts=$(echo \"$base\" | cut -d- -f1)\n159\t            name_ts=$(echo \"$base\" | sed -E 's/\\.ts$//')\n160\t            name=${name_ts#*-}\n161\t            expected_class=\"${name}${ts}\"\n162\t\n163\t            if ! grep -qE \"^export class ${expected_class}\\b\" \"$file\"; then\n164\t              echo \"::error file=$file::Class name mismatch — expected 'export class ${expected_class}' based on filename '$base'\"\n165\t              INVALID=1\n166\t            fi\n167\t          done < <(find apps -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' 2>/dev/null | sort)\n168\t\n169\t          if [ \"$INVALID\" -eq 1 ]; then\n170\t            exit 1\n171\t          fi\n172\t          echo \"All migration filenames and class names match convention.\"\n173\t\n174\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n175\t      # Check 3: no two migrations WITHIN A SINGLE SERVICE share a\n176\t      # timestamp. Each service owns its own `migrations` tracking table\n177\t      # (TypeORM runs migrations per DataSource, and every service\n178\t      # instantiates its own), so collisions only matter inside a service's\n179\t      # migration lineage. Collisions ACROSS services are not only allowed\n180\t      # but actively useful — they let a cross-cutting fix (e.g. NEW-H1,\n181\t      # which ships `1781900000000-ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz` to\n182\t      # 5 services simultaneously) advertise its relationship through a\n183\t      # shared timestamp that's easy to grep and correlate in incident\n184\t      # forensics. Within a single service, however, TypeORM orders the\n185\t      # migration list by this timestamp and a duplicate produces\n186\t      # non-deterministic ordering — the classic \"silently marks both as\n187\t      # applied even though only one actually runs\" production nightmare.\n188\t      #\n189\t      # Implementation note: we group by the service directory extracted\n190\t      # from the path (everything between `apps/` and `/src/...`), then\n191\t      # count duplicate timestamps inside each group independently.\n192\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n193\t      - name: Check for duplicate migration timestamps within each service\n194\t        run: |\n195\t          set -euo pipefail\n196\t          FAILED=0\n197\t          # Derive the list of services that actually own migrations.\n198\t          # ADR-030 day-one reset archived the pre-baseline chain into\n199\t          # apps/<svc>/src/(database/)migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/.\n200\t          # Those files share the 1800000000000-Baseline.ts timestamp by\n201\t          # design (the consolidated baseline collapsed them into a\n202\t          # single id) but are NEVER loaded by any DataSource's\n203\t          # `migrations: [...]` array. Excluding `.archive/` from this\n204\t          # scan is the structurally correct shape: TypeORM duplicate-\n205\t          # timestamp ordering hazard only matters for files the runner\n206\t          # actually loads.\n207\t          services=$(\n208\t            find apps -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' \\\n209\t              | sed -E 's|^apps/([^/]+)/.*|\\1|' \\\n210\t              | sort -u\n211\t          )\n212\t          for svc in $services; do\n213\t            dupes=$(\n214\t              find \"apps/${svc}\" -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' \\\n215\t                -exec basename {} \\; \\\n216\t                | cut -d- -f1 \\\n217\t                | sort \\\n218\t                | uniq -d\n219\t            )\n220\t            if [ -n \"$dupes\" ]; then\n221\t              echo \"::error::Duplicate migration timestamps detected within service '$svc':\"\n222\t              for ts in $dupes; do\n223\t                echo \"  $ts is used by:\"\n224\t                find \"apps/${svc}\" -type f -name \"${ts}-*.ts\" -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' | sed 's/^/    /'\n225\t              done\n226\t              FAILED=1\n227\t            fi\n228\t          done\n229\t          if [ \"$FAILED\" -eq 1 ]; then\n230\t            exit 1\n231\t          fi\n232\t          echo \"All migration timestamps are unique within each service.\"\n233\t\n234\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n235\t      # Check 4 — REMOVED in TRACK C (scope realignment).\n236\t      #\n237\t      # The previous revision compiled EVERY service that owns\n238\t      # migrations with `tsc --noEmit`, regardless of whether the PR\n239\t      # touched migration code. That made this gate fail on pre-existing\n240\t      # TS strict-null errors in totally unrelated libraries (e.g.\n241\t      # `libs/aquaculture-engines/water-chemistry/reagents.ts`) every\n242\t      # time anyone touched any `.entity.ts` file.\n243\t      #\n244\t      # Type-checking belongs in `ci-affected.yml`'s dedicated\n245\t      # `type-check` job which uses `nx affected -t type-check` —\n246\t      # only the services actually affected by a change get checked,\n247\t      # and the same gate applies uniformly to the whole codebase\n248\t      # (not just services that happen to own migrations).\n249\t      #\n250\t      # This workflow now focuses strictly on its named purpose:\n251\t      # migration filename / class-name / timestamp-uniqueness\n252\t      # conventions. The whole-service compile is no longer this\n253\t      # workflow's concern.\n254\t      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n255\t\n256\t  # ===================================================================\n257\t  # bootstrap-from-scratch — fresh-volume integration check.\n258\t  #\n259\t  # Runs the apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts test,\n260\t  # which spins up an empty Postgres testcontainer with the production\n261\t  # init-scripts mounted, runs every service's TypeORM migration chain\n262\t  # against it, and asserts the resulting schema state is what the\n263\t  # current entity surface expects.\n264\t  #\n265\t  # Why a separate job: this one needs a Docker daemon and ~5 minutes;\n266\t  # the conventions checks above are pure-shell <30s. Splitting keeps\n267\t  # PR feedback fast for the 99% case (filename/class-name regressions)\n268\t  # while still gating the rarer-but-catastrophic squash regression.\n269\t  #\n270\t  # GHA runners have docker preinstalled and the testcontainers library\n271\t  # autoreaches it; no extra setup beyond `npm ci`.\n272\t  # ===================================================================\n273\t  bootstrap-from-scratch:\n274\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n275\t    # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — bootstrap-from-scratch is now ~8 minutes\n276\t    # because the entity-surface coverage matrix runs per service AND\n277\t    # the SchemaDriftValidator runtime gate (Part A.4) iterates each\n278\t    # service's metadata. 12-minute ceiling gives ~50% headroom while\n279\t    # keeping the zombie-job blast radius bounded.\n280\t    timeout-minutes: 12\n281\t\n282\t    steps:\n283\t      - name: Checkout repository\n284\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n285\t\n286\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n287\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n288\t        with:\n289\t          node-version: '22'\n290\t          cache: 'npm'\n291\t\n292\t      - name: Install dependencies\n293\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n294\t\n295\t      # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — testcontainers image cache extension.\n296\t      # The Postgres pull (~250 MB compressed) dominates cold-start\n297\t      # time; restoring the image tarball from a cache key shaves\n298\t      # 60-90 seconds off every CI run that touches a path-filtered\n299\t      # surface. Cache key bound to the pinned digest in the spec\n300\t      # so a digest bump invalidates the cache (correct behaviour).\n301\t      - name: Restore docker image cache\n302\t        id: docker-image-cache\n303\t        uses: actions/cache@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0\n304\t        with:\n305\t          path: /tmp/docker-image-cache\n306\t          key: db-migration-check-pg16-timescaledb-${{ hashFiles('apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts') }}\n307\t\n308\t      - name: Load cached image (if present)\n309\t        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit == 'true'\n310\t        run: |\n311\t          if [ -f /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar ]; then\n312\t            docker load -i /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar\n313\t          fi\n314\t\n315\t      - name: Run bootstrap-from-scratch test\n316\t        run: npm run test:bootstrap\n317\t        env:\n318\t          # testcontainers reuse cuts seconds off subsequent runs and is\n319\t          # safe in single-job CI (we don't reuse across jobs).\n320\t          TESTCONTAINERS_REUSE_ENABLE: 'true'\n321\t\n322\t      - name: Save docker image cache\n323\t        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'\n324\t        run: |\n325\t          # Best-effort: save the timescaledb-ha image to the cache for\n326\t          # future runs. The grep|head pipeline can return empty if the\n327\t          # testcontainer cleaned up before this step runs (race), or\n328\t          # produce an `<none>:<none>` tag when an image is referenced\n329\t          # only by digest. Both yield `docker save` \"invalid reference\n330\t          # format\" and a non-zero exit code that fails the whole job.\n331\t          # The cache is an optimization, not a correctness gate — the\n332\t          # bootstrap test has already completed successfully by the\n333\t          # time this step runs. Guard the docker invocation so a cache\n334\t          # miss-save degrades to a no-op instead of failing CI.\n335\t          mkdir -p /tmp/docker-image-cache\n336\t          IMAGE_REF=$(docker images --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}}' \\\n337\t            | grep timescaledb-ha \\\n338\t            | grep -v '<none>' \\\n339\t            | head -1)\n340\t          if [ -z \"$IMAGE_REF\" ]; then\n341\t            echo \"No timescaledb-ha image with a usable tag found; skipping cache save.\"\n342\t            exit 0\n343\t          fi\n344\t          docker save -o /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar \"$IMAGE_REF\" \\\n345\t            || echo \"docker save failed for $IMAGE_REF; cache will be regenerated on next run.\"\n346\t\n347\t  # ===================================================================\n348\t  # tenant-clone-parity — Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 Part C.\n349\t  #\n350\t  # Asserts that per-tenant schema clones are a structurally faithful,\n351\t  # RLS-enabled mirror of every source-schema table. Runs against a\n352\t  # live Postgres service container (NOT testcontainers) after applying\n353\t  # the same aqua-db-migrate entrypoint used by production deploys.\n354\t  #\n355\t  # Why a separate job from bootstrap-from-scratch: the bootstrap spec\n356\t  # is hermetic (its own testcontainer); tenant-clone-parity targets\n357\t  # the running e2e Postgres so it can exercise the actual production\n358\t  # migration/provisioning path, not a hand-rolled mock. Splitting also lets\n359\t  # this job complete in ~3 minutes vs the bootstrap job's 8.\n360\t  # ===================================================================\n361\t  tenant-clone-parity:\n362\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n363\t    # 15 -> 20: the job now also provisions a real tenant through the\n364\t    # production provisioner (DATA-CRITICAL-010), which replays every\n365\t    # tenant-aware service's migration set into a fresh schema.\n366\t    timeout-minutes: 20\n367\t    env:\n368\t      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n369\t\n370\t    services:\n371\t      postgres:\n372\t        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n373\t        env:\n374\t          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n375\t          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n376\t          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n377\t        ports:\n378\t          - 5432:5432\n379\t        options: >-\n380\t          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n381\t          --health-interval 10s\n382\t          --health-timeout 5s\n383\t          --health-retries 10\n384\t\n385\t    steps:\n386\t      - name: Checkout repository\n387\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n388\t\n389\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n390\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n391\t        with:\n392\t          node-version: '22'\n393\t          cache: 'npm'\n394\t\n395\t      - name: Install dependencies\n396\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n397\t\n398\t      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n399\t        env:\n400\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n401\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n402\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n403\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n404\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n405\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n406\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n407\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n408\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n409\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n410\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n411\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n412\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n413\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n414\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n415\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n416\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n417\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n418\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n419\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n420\t        run: |\n421\t          npx ts-node \\\n422\t            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n423\t            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n424\t            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n425\t\n426\t      - name: Run tenant-clone-parity test\n427\t        run: npm run test:tenant-clone\n428\t        env:\n429\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n430\t\n431\t      # DATA-CRITICAL-010: the gate the finding's own closure criterion asks\n432\t      # for. Until this step, NO spec in the repository entered the PROVISION\n433\t      # branch — so \"a super admin creates a tenant\", the first thing the pilot\n434\t      # does, was the one path CI never executed, and a Baseline corpus that\n435\t      # could not be replayed into any tenant schema stayed green for four\n436\t      # months. It runs here because the database above has just been migrated\n437\t      # by the real db-migrate entry point, which is the same binary the spec\n438\t      # then invokes in `tenant-schema-provisioner --once` mode. The tenant it\n439\t      # creates is left in place on purpose: schema-invariants B.5a asserts\n440\t      # every tenant-scoped schema has at least one clone, and with\n441\t      # tenant-clone-parity dropping its fixture in afterAll that assertion was\n442\t      # passing against zero tenants on every run.\n443\t      - name: Provision a real tenant through the production provisioner\n444\t        env:\n445\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n446\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n447\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n448\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n449\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n450\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n451\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n452\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n453\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n454\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n455\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n456\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n457\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n458\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n459\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n460\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n461\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n462\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n463\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n464\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n465\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n466\t        run: npm run test:tenant-provisioning\n467\t\n468\t      # SENSOR-MEDIUM-052: CLAUDE.md named this spec as the per-PR schema\n469\t      # invariant gate, but nothing invoked it — this workflow only listed it as\n470\t      # a path filter. It runs here because the database above has just been\n471\t      # migrated by the real db-migrate entry point, which is exactly the live\n472\t      # DDL the spec asserts against (public/shared layout, per-service\n473\t      # cross-tenant `schema:` placement).\n474\t      - name: Run schema invariants against the migrated database\n475\t        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n476\t        env:\n477\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n478\t\n479\t  # ===================================================================\n480\t  # schema-invariants — physical schema layout gate (FARM-MEDIUM-303).\n481\t  #\n482\t  # `e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts` named this\n483\t  # workflow as its home since the 2026-04-14 public-schema teardown,\n484\t  # but only ever appeared in the `paths:` filters above — it triggered\n485\t  # the workflow and was then never executed by any step. A spec that\n486\t  # nothing runs is not a gate: the B.1/B.2 assertions (an @Entity that\n487\t  # forgets `schema:` lands in `public`; a declared schema that does not\n488\t  # match the physical one) were silently uncovered the whole time,\n489\t  # which is exactly the multi-tenant isolation regression the teardown\n490\t  # plan was built to prevent.\n491\t  #\n492\t  # Why its own job rather than a step on tenant-clone-parity: the two\n493\t  # specs assert different things and must attribute their failures\n494\t  # separately — \"which invariant broke\" is readable from the job name,\n495\t  # matching how every other gate in this workflow is structured.\n496\t  #\n497\t  # Preconditions the spec needs, and where they come from:\n498\t  #   - every service schema present + owned by `<svc>_service`\n499\t  #     (assertion B.4) — created by aqua-db-migrate's platform\n500\t  #     bootstrap stage 003, which the migrate step below runs;\n501\t  #   - `shared` holding exactly the canonical cross-service tables\n502\t  #     (B.3) — bootstrap stage 006.\n503\t  # B.5a (tenant clones exist) is vacuous here because a freshly\n504\t  # migrated DB has no tenants; that is by the spec's own design — the\n505\t  # non-vacuous version of that check is tenant-clone-parity above,\n506\t  # which provisions a tenant schema and asserts against it directly.\n507\t  # ===================================================================\n508\t  schema-invariants:\n509\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n510\t    timeout-minutes: 10\n511\t    env:\n512\t      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n513\t\n514\t    services:\n515\t      postgres:\n516\t        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n517\t        env:\n518\t          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n519\t          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n520\t          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n521\t        ports:\n522\t          - 5432:5432\n523\t        options: >-\n524\t          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n525\t          --health-interval 10s\n526\t          --health-timeout 5s\n527\t          --health-retries 10\n528\t\n529\t    steps:\n530\t      - name: Checkout repository\n531\t        uses: actions/checkout@9c091bb21b7c1c1d1991bb908d89e4e9dddfe3e0 # v7.0.0\n532\t\n533\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n534\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n535\t        with:\n536\t          node-version: '22'\n537\t          cache: 'npm'\n538\t\n539\t      - name: Install dependencies\n540\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n541\t\n542\t      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n543\t        env:\n544\t          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n545\t          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n546\t          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n547\t          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n548\t          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n549\t          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n550\t          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n551\t          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n552\t          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n553\t          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n554\t          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n555\t          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n556\t          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n557\t          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n558\t          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n559\t          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n560\t          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n561\t          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n562\t          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n563\t          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n564\t        run: |\n565\t          npx ts-node \\\n566\t            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n567\t            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n568\t            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n569\t\n570\t      - name: Run schema-invariants test\n571\t        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n572\t        env:\n573\t          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n574\t\n575\t  # ===================================================================\n576\t  # migration-deletion-witness — pre-merge gate.\n577\t  #\n578\t  # Catches the silent-regression class where a migration file (or\n579\t  # an init-script with table-creating SQL) is deleted without\n580\t  # either a sibling-service migration carrying the same CREATE\n581\t  # forward, an init-script equivalent, or an explicit\n582\t  # `DELETE-OK: <reason>` line in the PR body. See\n583\t  # tools/gates/migration-deletion-witness.ts docblock for the full\n584\t  # algorithm.\n585\t  # ===================================================================\n586\t  migration-deletion-witness:\n587\t    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n588\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n589\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n590\t\n591\t    steps:\n592\t      - name: Checkout repository\n593\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n594\t        with:\n595\t          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n596\t          fetch-depth: 0\n597\t\n598\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n599\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n600\t        with:\n601\t          node-version: '22'\n602\t          cache: 'npm'\n603\t\n604\t      - name: Install dependencies\n605\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n606\t\n607\t      - name: Run migration-deletion-witness gate\n608\t        env:\n609\t          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n610\t          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n611\t          HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\n612\t        run: npm run gates:migration-deletion-witness\n613\t\n614\t  # ===================================================================\n615\t  # entity-diff-witness — Faz 1.3 of day-one baseline reset.\n616\t  #\n617\t  # Catches the silent-regression class where an `@Entity()` file is\n618\t  # edited (column add/drop, type change, nullability flip, FK add,\n619\t  # @Check change) WITHOUT a matching migration landing in the same PR.\n620\t  # This is the upstream gate that prevents the 2026-04 drift archaeology\n621\t  # (14 services' Align*EntitySurface / Heal*Drift / Replay* chains).\n622\t  #\n623\t  # The PR-body waiver `ENTITY-DIFF-OK: <service> — <reason>` is honoured\n624\t  # for genuine no-DDL-impact edits (typing fix, JSDoc, etc.) but the\n625\t  # waiver carries CODEOWNERS review from database-reviewer.\n626\t  # ===================================================================\n627\t  entity-diff-witness:\n628\t    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n629\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n630\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n631\t\n632\t    steps:\n633\t      - name: Checkout repository\n634\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n635\t        with:\n636\t          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n637\t          fetch-depth: 0\n638\t\n639\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n640\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n641\t        with:\n642\t          node-version: '22'\n643\t          cache: 'npm'\n644\t\n645\t      - name: Install dependencies\n646\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n647\t\n648\t      - name: Run entity-diff-witness gate\n649\t        env:\n650\t          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n651\t          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n652\t        run: npm run gates:entity-diff-witness -- --diff-base \"$BASE_SHA\"\n653\t\n654\t  # ===================================================================\n655\t  # migration-immutability-witness — shipped-migration in-place-edit guard.\n656\t  #\n657\t  # Catches the silent-drift class where an ALREADY-SHIPPED timestamped\n658\t  # migration file is edited in place instead of being superseded by a new\n659\t  # forward migration. TypeORM records the ledger by migration NAME, so an\n660\t  # edit to an already-applied migration never re-runs — the 2026-06 tenant-\n661\t  # provisioning incident (1800400000000 hand-edited to add lease columns +\n662\t  # an auth.tenants REVOKE that silently never landed; every POST /api/v1/\n663\t  # tenants then 500'd with `column \"leaseToken\" does not exist`).\n664\t  #\n665\t  # The PR-body waiver `MIGRATION-IMMUTABLE-OK: <filename> — <reason>` is\n666\t  # honoured for genuinely content-neutral edits (comment/lint-only) and\n667\t  # carries CODEOWNERS review from database-reviewer.\n668\t  # ===================================================================\n669\t  migration-immutability-witness:\n670\t    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n671\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n672\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n673\t\n674\t    steps:\n675\t      - name: Checkout repository\n676\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n677\t        with:\n678\t          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n679\t          fetch-depth: 0\n680\t\n681\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n682\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n683\t        with:\n684\t          node-version: '22'\n685\t          cache: 'npm'\n686\t\n687\t      - name: Install dependencies\n688\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n689\t\n690\t      - name: Run migration-immutability-witness gate\n691\t        env:\n692\t          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n693\t          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n694\t        run: npm run gates:migration-immutability-witness -- --diff-base \"$BASE_SHA\"\n695\t\n696\t  # ===================================================================\n697\t  # schema-drift-registration — AST gate.\n698\t  #\n699\t  # Asserts every canonical service's app.module.ts registers\n700\t  # SchemaDriftModule.forRoot({ serviceName: '<svc>' }). Without\n701\t  # registration, the runtime drift validator never instantiates and\n702\t  # silently disarms ADR-012's third defense layer. Static gate runs\n703\t  # in seconds; no DB needed.\n704\t  # ===================================================================\n705\t  schema-drift-registration:\n706\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n707\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n708\t\n709\t    steps:\n710\t      - name: Checkout repository\n711\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n712\t\n713\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n714\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n715\t        with:\n716\t          node-version: '22'\n717\t          cache: 'npm'\n718\t\n719\t      - name: Install dependencies\n720\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n721\t\n722\t      - name: Run schema-drift-registration gate\n723\t        run: npm run gates:schema-drift-registration\n724\t\n725\t  # ===================================================================\n726\t  # signals-manifest — boot invariant signal contract gate.\n727\t  #\n728\t  # Asserts required-signals.yaml stays aligned with the canonical\n729\t  # BOOT_INVARIANT_SIGNALS constants, compose services, SchemaDriftModule\n730\t  # registrations, and EventBusModule registrations.\n731\t  # ===================================================================\n732\t  signals-manifest:\n733\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n734\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n735\t\n736\t    steps:\n737\t      - name: Checkout repository\n738\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n739\t\n740\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n741\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n742\t        with:\n743\t          node-version: '22'\n744\t          cache: 'npm'\n745\t\n746\t      - name: Install dependencies\n747\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n748\t\n749\t      - name: Run signals manifest gate\n750\t        run: npm run gates:signals-manifest\n751\t\n752\t  # ===================================================================\n753\t  # criticality-manifest — service criticality ↔ compose service gate.\n754\t  #\n755\t  # Runs beside signals-manifest so deploy health policy and boot-signal\n756\t  # expectations drift together instead of independently.\n757\t  # ===================================================================\n758\t  criticality-manifest:\n759\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n760\t    timeout-minutes: 5\n761\t\n762\t    steps:\n763\t      - name: Checkout repository\n764\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n765\t\n766\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n767\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n768\t        with:\n769\t          node-version: '22'\n770\t          cache: 'npm'\n771\t\n772\t      - name: Install dependencies\n773\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n774\t\n775\t      - name: Run criticality manifest gate\n776\t        run: npm run gates:criticality-manifest\n777\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9ef735c0-3ced-4041-a188-30d901fc073d","uuid":"6ed17c29-fb98-45ca-af17-cecbd8050b27","timestamp":"2026-10-08T04:19:55.269Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-062d5da63359/.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml","content":"name: Database Migration Check\n\n# =============================================================================\n# Database Migration Check — TypeORM Migration Validation\n#\n# PURPOSE\n# -------\n# Fast, hermetic validation of the TypeORM migration files that actually\n# ship with this codebase, with no database, no NX build, and no docker.\n# The previous revision of this workflow tried to:\n#\n#   1. Build gateway-api with `npx nx build gateway-api`, then run\n#      `typeorm migration:run -d dist/apps/gateway-api/src/data-source.js`.\n#      Neither gateway-api nor any other service owns a `data-source.ts`\n#      file — migrations are registered as class references inside\n#      `TypeOrmModule.forRoot({ migrations: [...] })` and executed at\n#      service-boot time by `MigrationRunnerService`, not by the typeorm\n#      CLI. The step has never worked in this codebase.\n#\n#   2. Run Flyway validate against `database/migrations/core/**` and\n#      `database/migrations/modules/**`. The Flyway tree is a\n#      vestigial scaffolding experiment that was never wired into\n#      the runtime — nothing in the application code references\n#      `database/migrations/**` (every service registers its own\n#      TypeORM migration classes via TypeOrmModule.forRoot per the\n#      WHAT THIS WORKFLOW CHECKS NOW section below). The Flyway\n#      job was removed when this workflow was rewritten.\n#\n#      DATA-LOW-002 cure: a previous revision of this comment\n#      claimed \"9 of the 13 SQL files in that tree are empty (0\n#      bytes)\". Direct file-size check at 2026-04-29 showed every\n#      `database/migrations/core/V*.sql` carried 281–2491 bytes\n#      and every `database/migrations/modules/<svc>/V*.sql` carried\n#      762–5598 bytes. None empty. The misclaim was a documentation-\n#      rot artifact from a much earlier iteration of the tree —\n#      kept here as a historical note in case future maintainers\n#      grep for the old phrasing.\n#\n#      The Flyway tree itself is left in place so the git history\n#      stays intact and anyone looking for the old Flyway design\n#      can still find it. DATA-HIGH-001 (the agent's separate\n#      tracking finding for the orphan tree) covers the eventual\n#      removal — until that lands, this workflow neither validates\n#      the tree nor relies on it.\n#\n# Beyond that, the `npx nx build` step hung silently for hours on every\n# run because the Windows-generated `package-lock.json` has no\n# `@nx/nx-linux-x64-gnu` binary; without it NX hangs until the 6-hour\n# GitHub Actions runner timeout. Five zombie runs piled up at\n# 09:36Z, 12:27Z, 13:37Z, 13:53Z, and 14:28Z on 2026-04-07 before this\n# rewrite landed.\n#\n# WHAT THIS WORKFLOW CHECKS NOW\n# -----------------------------\n# The real migration path in this codebase is\n# `apps/<service>/src/**/migrations/<timestamp>-<Name>.ts`, one class\n# per file, registered in the service's AppModule. The checks below\n# match that reality:\n#\n#   1. Filename convention — every migration file name must match\n#      `<13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts`.\n#   2. Class name consistency — the exported class inside must be\n#      named `<PascalCaseName><timestamp>` and match the file name.\n#   3. Global timestamp uniqueness — no two migrations anywhere in the\n#      repo may share the same timestamp. Collisions would produce\n#      undefined ordering under TypeORM's migration runner.\n#   4. Syntax / type check — each migration file compiles under\n#      `tsc --noEmit` against the service's `tsconfig.build.json` so\n#      structural errors (missing imports, bad decorator usage, typos\n#      in table names as SQL string literals — the latter caught only\n#      at runtime but the former all caught here) surface at PR time\n#      instead of at deploy time.\n#\n# The job finishes in well under a minute and has no external\n# dependencies: no postgres, no docker, no native binaries, no NX.\n# It cannot hang.\n#\n# Real integration-level migration validation — i.e. \"does the\n# migration actually run against a database\" — happens at deploy time\n# via `MigrationRunnerService.onApplicationBootstrap()` and the\n# deploy workflow's `Verify deployment` health check. Two different\n# quality gates at two different layers, neither duplicating the\n# other's work.\n#\n# SEC-CI-002: All third-party actions pinned to full commit SHAs.\n# =============================================================================\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    paths:\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/*.entity.ts'\n      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n  push:\n    branches: [main, develop]\n    paths:\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n  # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — nightly run catches drift that touched zero\n  # path-filtered files but introduced runtime drift (e.g. an\n  # init-script change merged through a different filename, or a\n  # silent platform-image bump in docker-compose.infra.yml). 03:00\n  # UTC chosen to follow the post-midnight infra-quiet window in\n  # docs/runbooks/.\n  schedule:\n    - cron: '0 3 * * *'\n\n# SEC-CI-021: Minimum token permissions.\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  typeorm-migration-validation:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    # Bounded budget — the checks take ~30 seconds on a cold runner.\n    # 10 minutes is a generous ceiling that still guarantees no zombies.\n    timeout-minutes: 10\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n      # Check 1 + 2: filename convention and class name consistency.\n      # Pure shell — no node_modules needed. Fails fast on the first bad\n      # file with an inline GitHub annotation so the PR reviewer can\n      # click straight to the offending line.\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n      - name: Validate migration filename and class name conventions\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          INVALID=0\n          while IFS= read -r file; do\n            base=$(basename \"$file\")\n            # Expected: <13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts\n            if ! echo \"$base\" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{13}-[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*\\.ts$'; then\n              echo \"::error file=$file::Invalid migration filename: '$base' (expected <13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts)\"\n              INVALID=1\n              continue\n            fi\n\n            # Derive the expected class name from the file name:\n            #   \"1781900000000-ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz.ts\"\n            # → class \"ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz1781900000000\"\n            ts=$(echo \"$base\" | cut -d- -f1)\n            name_ts=$(echo \"$base\" | sed -E 's/\\.ts$//')\n            name=${name_ts#*-}\n            expected_class=\"${name}${ts}\"\n\n            if ! grep -qE \"^export class ${expected_class}\\b\" \"$file\"; then\n              echo \"::error file=$file::Class name mismatch — expected 'export class ${expected_class}' based on filename '$base'\"\n              INVALID=1\n            fi\n          done < <(find apps -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' 2>/dev/null | sort)\n\n          if [ \"$INVALID\" -eq 1 ]; then\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"All migration filenames and class names match convention.\"\n\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n      # Check 3: no two migrations WITHIN A SINGLE SERVICE share a\n      # timestamp. Each service owns its own `migrations` tracking table\n      # (TypeORM runs migrations per DataSource, and every service\n      # instantiates its own), so collisions only matter inside a service's\n      # migration lineage. Collisions ACROSS services are not only allowed\n      # but actively useful — they let a cross-cutting fix (e.g. NEW-H1,\n      # which ships `1781900000000-ConvertAuditColumnsToTimestamptz` to\n      # 5 services simultaneously) advertise its relationship through a\n      # shared timestamp that's easy to grep and correlate in incident\n      # forensics. Within a single service, however, TypeORM orders the\n      # migration list by this timestamp and a duplicate produces\n      # non-deterministic ordering — the classic \"silently marks both as\n      # applied even though only one actually runs\" production nightmare.\n      #\n      # Implementation note: we group by the service directory extracted\n      # from the path (everything between `apps/` and `/src/...`), then\n      # count duplicate timestamps inside each group independently.\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n      - name: Check for duplicate migration timestamps within each service\n        run: |\n          set -euo pipefail\n          FAILED=0\n          # Derive the list of services that actually own migrations.\n          # ADR-030 day-one reset archived the pre-baseline chain into\n          # apps/<svc>/src/(database/)migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/.\n          # Those files share the 1800000000000-Baseline.ts timestamp by\n          # design (the consolidated baseline collapsed them into a\n          # single id) but are NEVER loaded by any DataSource's\n          # `migrations: [...]` array. Excluding `.archive/` from this\n          # scan is the structurally correct shape: TypeORM duplicate-\n          # timestamp ordering hazard only matters for files the runner\n          # actually loads.\n          services=$(\n            find apps -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' \\\n              | sed -E 's|^apps/([^/]+)/.*|\\1|' \\\n              | sort -u\n          )\n          for svc in $services; do\n            dupes=$(\n              find \"apps/${svc}\" -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' \\\n                -exec basename {} \\; \\\n                | cut -d- -f1 \\\n                | sort \\\n                | uniq -d\n            )\n            if [ -n \"$dupes\" ]; then\n              echo \"::error::Duplicate migration timestamps detected within service '$svc':\"\n              for ts in $dupes; do\n                echo \"  $ts is used by:\"\n                find \"apps/${svc}\" -type f -name \"${ts}-*.ts\" -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' | sed 's/^/    /'\n              done\n              FAILED=1\n            fi\n          done\n          if [ \"$FAILED\" -eq 1 ]; then\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"All migration timestamps are unique within each service.\"\n\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n      # Check 4 — REMOVED in TRACK C (scope realignment).\n      #\n      # The previous revision compiled EVERY service that owns\n      # migrations with `tsc --noEmit`, regardless of whether the PR\n      # touched migration code. That made this gate fail on pre-existing\n      # TS strict-null errors in totally unrelated libraries (e.g.\n      # `libs/aquaculture-engines/water-chemistry/reagents.ts`) every\n      # time anyone touched any `.entity.ts` file.\n      #\n      # Type-checking belongs in `ci-affected.yml`'s dedicated\n      # `type-check` job which uses `nx affected -t type-check` —\n      # only the services actually affected by a change get checked,\n      # and the same gate applies uniformly to the whole codebase\n      # (not just services that happen to own migrations).\n      #\n      # This workflow now focuses strictly on its named purpose:\n      # migration filename / class-name / timestamp-uniqueness\n      # conventions. The whole-service compile is no longer this\n      # workflow's concern.\n      # -------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # bootstrap-from-scratch — fresh-volume integration check.\n  #\n  # Runs the apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts test,\n  # which spins up an empty Postgres testcontainer with the production\n  # init-scripts mounted, runs every service's TypeORM migration chain\n  # against it, and asserts the resulting schema state is what the\n  # current entity surface expects.\n  #\n  # Why a separate job: this one needs a Docker daemon and ~5 minutes;\n  # the conventions checks above are pure-shell <30s. Splitting keeps\n  # PR feedback fast for the 99% case (filename/class-name regressions)\n  # while still gating the rarer-but-catastrophic squash regression.\n  #\n  # GHA runners have docker preinstalled and the testcontainers library\n  # autoreaches it; no extra setup beyond `npm ci`.\n  # ===================================================================\n  bootstrap-from-scratch:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — bootstrap-from-scratch is now ~8 minutes\n    # because the entity-surface coverage matrix runs per service AND\n    # the SchemaDriftValidator runtime gate (Part A.4) iterates each\n    # service's metadata. 12-minute ceiling gives ~50% headroom while\n    # keeping the zombie-job blast radius bounded.\n    timeout-minutes: 12\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — testcontainers image cache extension.\n      # The Postgres pull (~250 MB compressed) dominates cold-start\n      # time; restoring the image tarball from a cache key shaves\n      # 60-90 seconds off every CI run that touches a path-filtered\n      # surface. Cache key bound to the pinned digest in the spec\n      # so a digest bump invalidates the cache (correct behaviour).\n      - name: Restore docker image cache\n        id: docker-image-cache\n        uses: actions/cache@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0\n        with:\n          path: /tmp/docker-image-cache\n          key: db-migration-check-pg16-timescaledb-${{ hashFiles('apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts') }}\n\n      - name: Load cached image (if present)\n        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit == 'true'\n        run: |\n          if [ -f /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar ]; then\n            docker load -i /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar\n          fi\n\n      - name: Run bootstrap-from-scratch test\n        run: npm run test:bootstrap\n        env:\n          # testcontainers reuse cuts seconds off subsequent runs and is\n          # safe in single-job CI (we don't reuse across jobs).\n          TESTCONTAINERS_REUSE_ENABLE: 'true'\n\n      - name: Save docker image cache\n        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'\n        run: |\n          # Best-effort: save the timescaledb-ha image to the cache for\n          # future runs. The grep|head pipeline can return empty if the\n          # testcontainer cleaned up before this step runs (race), or\n          # produce an `<none>:<none>` tag when an image is referenced\n          # only by digest. Both yield `docker save` \"invalid reference\n          # format\" and a non-zero exit code that fails the whole job.\n          # The cache is an optimization, not a correctness gate — the\n          # bootstrap test has already completed successfully by the\n          # time this step runs. Guard the docker invocation so a cache\n          # miss-save degrades to a no-op instead of failing CI.\n          mkdir -p /tmp/docker-image-cache\n          IMAGE_REF=$(docker images --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}}' \\\n            | grep timescaledb-ha \\\n            | grep -v '<none>' \\\n            | head -1)\n          if [ -z \"$IMAGE_REF\" ]; then\n            echo \"No timescaledb-ha image with a usable tag found; skipping cache save.\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n          docker save -o /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar \"$IMAGE_REF\" \\\n            || echo \"docker save failed for $IMAGE_REF; cache will be regenerated on next run.\"\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # tenant-clone-parity — Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 Part C.\n  #\n  # Asserts that per-tenant schema clones are a structurally faithful,\n  # RLS-enabled mirror of every source-schema table. Runs against a\n  # live Postgres service container (NOT testcontainers) after applying\n  # the same aqua-db-migrate entrypoint used by production deploys.\n  #\n  # Why a separate job from bootstrap-from-scratch: the bootstrap spec\n  # is hermetic (its own testcontainer); tenant-clone-parity targets\n  # the running e2e Postgres so it can exercise the actual production\n  # migration/provisioning path, not a hand-rolled mock. Splitting also lets\n  # this job complete in ~3 minutes vs the bootstrap job's 8.\n  # ===================================================================\n  tenant-clone-parity:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    # 15 -> 20: the job now also provisions a real tenant through the\n    # production provisioner (DATA-CRITICAL-010), which replays every\n    # tenant-aware service's migration set into a fresh schema.\n    timeout-minutes: 20\n    env:\n      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n\n    services:\n      postgres:\n        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n        env:\n          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n        ports:\n          - 5432:5432\n        options: >-\n          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n          --health-interval 10s\n          --health-timeout 5s\n          --health-retries 10\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n        env:\n          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n        run: |\n          npx ts-node \\\n            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n\n      - name: Run tenant-clone-parity test\n        run: npm run test:tenant-clone\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n\n      # DATA-CRITICAL-010: the gate the finding's own closure criterion asks\n      # for. Until this step, NO spec in the repository entered the PROVISION\n      # branch — so \"a super admin creates a tenant\", the first thing the pilot\n      # does, was the one path CI never executed, and a Baseline corpus that\n      # could not be replayed into any tenant schema stayed green for four\n      # months. It runs here because the database above has just been migrated\n      # by the real db-migrate entry point, which is the same binary the spec\n      # then invokes in `tenant-schema-provisioner --once` mode. The tenant it\n      # creates is left in place on purpose: schema-invariants B.5a asserts\n      # every tenant-scoped schema has at least one clone, and with\n      # tenant-clone-parity dropping its fixture in afterAll that assertion was\n      # passing against zero tenants on every run.\n      - name: Provision a real tenant through the production provisioner\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n        run: npm run test:tenant-provisioning\n\n      # SENSOR-MEDIUM-052: CLAUDE.md named this spec as the per-PR schema\n      # invariant gate, but nothing invoked it — this workflow only listed it as\n      # a path filter. It runs here because the database above has just been\n      # migrated by the real db-migrate entry point, which is exactly the live\n      # DDL the spec asserts against (public/shared layout, per-service\n      # cross-tenant `schema:` placement).\n      - name: Run schema invariants against the migrated database\n        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # schema-invariants — physical schema layout gate (FARM-MEDIUM-303).\n  #\n  # `e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts` named this\n  # workflow as its home since the 2026-04-14 public-schema teardown,\n  # but only ever appeared in the `paths:` filters above — it triggered\n  # the workflow and was then never executed by any step. A spec that\n  # nothing runs is not a gate: the B.1/B.2 assertions (an @Entity that\n  # forgets `schema:` lands in `public`; a declared schema that does not\n  # match the physical one) were silently uncovered the whole time,\n  # which is exactly the multi-tenant isolation regression the teardown\n  # plan was built to prevent.\n  #\n  # Why its own job rather than a step on tenant-clone-parity: the two\n  # specs assert different things and must attribute their failures\n  # separately — \"which invariant broke\" is readable from the job name,\n  # matching how every other gate in this workflow is structured.\n  #\n  # Preconditions the spec needs, and where they come from:\n  #   - every service schema present + owned by `<svc>_service`\n  #     (assertion B.4) — created by aqua-db-migrate's platform\n  #     bootstrap stage 003, which the migrate step below runs;\n  #   - `shared` holding exactly the canonical cross-service tables\n  #     (B.3) — bootstrap stage 006.\n  # B.5a (tenant clones exist) is vacuous here because a freshly\n  # migrated DB has no tenants; that is by the spec's own design — the\n  # non-vacuous version of that check is tenant-clone-parity above,\n  # which provisions a tenant schema and asserts against it directly.\n  # ===================================================================\n  schema-invariants:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 10\n    env:\n      AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE: aquaculture-test\n\n    services:\n      postgres:\n        image: timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7\n        env:\n          POSTGRES_DB: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_USER: aquaculture\n          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n        ports:\n          - 5432:5432\n        options: >-\n          --health-cmd \"pg_isready -U aquaculture\"\n          --health-interval 10s\n          --health-timeout 5s\n          --health-retries 10\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@9c091bb21b7c1c1d1991bb908d89e4e9dddfe3e0 # v7.0.0\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run aqua-db-migrate against test DB\n        env:\n          DATABASE_HOST: localhost\n          DATABASE_PORT: '5432'\n          DATABASE_USER: aquaculture\n          DATABASE_PASSWORD: aquaculture-test\n          DATABASE_NAME: aquaculture\n          AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n          CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS: ${{ env.AQUA_TEST_DB_VALUE }}\n        run: |\n          npx ts-node \\\n            --project apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.app.json \\\n            -r tsconfig-paths/register \\\n            apps/db-migrate/src/main.ts\n\n      - name: Run schema-invariants test\n        run: npm run test:schema-invariants\n        env:\n          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aquaculture:aquaculture-test@localhost:5432/aquaculture\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # migration-deletion-witness — pre-merge gate.\n  #\n  # Catches the silent-regression class where a migration file (or\n  # an init-script with table-creating SQL) is deleted without\n  # either a sibling-service migration carrying the same CREATE\n  # forward, an init-script equivalent, or an explicit\n  # `DELETE-OK: <reason>` line in the PR body. See\n  # tools/gates/migration-deletion-witness.ts docblock for the full\n  # algorithm.\n  # ===================================================================\n  migration-deletion-witness:\n    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 5\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n        with:\n          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run migration-deletion-witness gate\n        env:\n          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n          HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}\n        run: npm run gates:migration-deletion-witness\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # entity-diff-witness — Faz 1.3 of day-one baseline reset.\n  #\n  # Catches the silent-regression class where an `@Entity()` file is\n  # edited (column add/drop, type change, nullability flip, FK add,\n  # @Check change) WITHOUT a matching migration landing in the same PR.\n  # This is the upstream gate that prevents the 2026-04 drift archaeology\n  # (14 services' Align*EntitySurface / Heal*Drift / Replay* chains).\n  #\n  # The PR-body waiver `ENTITY-DIFF-OK: <service> — <reason>` is honoured\n  # for genuine no-DDL-impact edits (typing fix, JSDoc, etc.) but the\n  # waiver carries CODEOWNERS review from database-reviewer.\n  # ===================================================================\n  entity-diff-witness:\n    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 5\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n        with:\n          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run entity-diff-witness gate\n        env:\n          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n        run: npm run gates:entity-diff-witness -- --diff-base \"$BASE_SHA\"\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # migration-immutability-witness — shipped-migration in-place-edit guard.\n  #\n  # Catches the silent-drift class where an ALREADY-SHIPPED timestamped\n  # migration file is edited in place instead of being superseded by a new\n  # forward migration. TypeORM records the ledger by migration NAME, so an\n  # edit to an already-applied migration never re-runs — the 2026-06 tenant-\n  # provisioning incident (1800400000000 hand-edited to add lease columns +\n  # an auth.tenants REVOKE that silently never landed; every POST /api/v1/\n  # tenants then 500'd with `column \"leaseToken\" does not exist`).\n  #\n  # The PR-body waiver `MIGRATION-IMMUTABLE-OK: <filename> — <reason>` is\n  # honoured for genuinely content-neutral edits (comment/lint-only) and\n  # carries CODEOWNERS review from database-reviewer.\n  # ===================================================================\n  migration-immutability-witness:\n    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 5\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n        with:\n          # Need the merge-base for git diff name-status.\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run migration-immutability-witness gate\n        env:\n          PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}\n          BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.base.sha }}\n        run: npm run gates:migration-immutability-witness -- --diff-base \"$BASE_SHA\"\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # schema-drift-registration — AST gate.\n  #\n  # Asserts every canonical service's app.module.ts registers\n  # SchemaDriftModule.forRoot({ serviceName: '<svc>' }). Without\n  # registration, the runtime drift validator never instantiates and\n  # silently disarms ADR-012's third defense layer. Static gate runs\n  # in seconds; no DB needed.\n  # ===================================================================\n  schema-drift-registration:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 5\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      - name: Run schema-drift-registration gate\n        run: npm run gates:schema-drift-registration\n\n  # ===================================================================\n  # signals-manifest — boot invariant signal contract gate.\n  #\n  # Asserts required-signals.yaml stays aligned with the canonical\n  # BOOT_INVARIANT_SIGNALS constants, compose services, SchemaDriftModule\n  # registrations, and EventBusModule registrations.\n  # ===================================================================\n  signals-manifest:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 5\n\n    steps:\n      - '../1801800000000-BackfillTankBatchCurrentQuantityMirror';\n\n/**\n * Contract cover for the currentQuantity/currentBiomassKg mirror reconciliation\n * (ORPHAN-HIGH-276) — the completion of the 719-vs-900 data repair. London-school\n * SHAPE assertions (typed jest.Mocked<QueryRunner>, no live DB): the risks are\n * (a) running against a per-tenant table absent in the source schema, and\n * (b) mutating rows that are already consistent.\n */\ndescribe('BackfillTankBatchCurrentQuantityMirror1801800000000', () => {\n  let mockQueryRunner: jest.Mocked<QueryRunner>;\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    ({ mockQueryRunner } = createMockDataSource());\n    mockQueryRunner.query.mockResolvedValue(undefined);\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => jest.restoreAllMocks());\n\n  async function upSql(): Promise<string> {\n    await new BackfillTankBatchCurrentQuantityMirror1801800000000().up(mockQueryRunner);\n    return String(mockQueryRunner.query.mock.calls[0]![0]);\n  }\n\n  it('no-ops in schemas that do not own the per-tenant tank_batches table', async () => {\n    const sql = await upSql();\n    expect(sql).toContain(\"to_regclass(current_schema() || '.tank_batches') IS NULL\");\n    expect(sql).toContain('RETURN;');\n  });\n\n  it('mirrors currentQuantity/currentBiomassKg to the authoritative totals', async () => {\n    const sql = await upSql();\n    expect(sql).toMatch(/\"currentQuantity\"\\s*=\\s*\"totalQuantity\"/);\n    expect(sql).toMatch(/\"currentBiomassKg\"\\s*=\\s*\"totalBiomassKg\"/);\n  });\n\n  it('only touches divergent rows (idempotent — IS DISTINCT FROM covers NULL mirrors)', async () => {\n    const sql = await upSql();\n    expect(sql).toMatch(/\"currentQuantity\"\\s+IS DISTINCT FROM\\s+\"totalQuantity\"/);\n    expect(sql).toMatch(/\"currentBiomassKg\"\\s+IS DISTINCT FROM\\s+\"totalBiomassKg\"/);\n  });\n\n  it('down() is a deliberate no-op — the mirror is derived, not restorable', async () => {\n    await new BackfillTankBatchCurrentQuantityMirror1801800000000().down(mockQueryRunner);\n    expect(mockQueryRunner.query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n","numLines":52,"startLine":1,"totalLines":52}}}
